Title: Kepos chariton, toutesti, Biblion periechon ten periodon tou timiou Xylou tou zoiopoiou Staurou, tou en tei hierai kai basilikei Monei tou Xeropotamou, tei ousei en toi hagionymoi Orei tou Athonos kai alla diaphora
Creator: Konstantinos Dapontes, called Kaisarios as a monk (author); Gabriel Sophocleous (editor)
Publisher: Athens (title page: Athenesin; the host record names the press of Hermes); the editor's preface is dated 1880
Publication Date: 1880 (host record; the editor's preface at about OCR line 1111 is dated at Athens, 1880)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1880); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: grc
Source: archive.org item keposcharitontou00dapo, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (730,833) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1880, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair: noisy polytonic Greek, readable. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Kaisarios Dapontes's own book on the journey of the relic of the True Cross of the Monastery of Xeropotamou on Mount Athos, 'and other things', printed for the first time. The title page at about OCR lines 285-345 names him as Konstantinos Dapontes, renamed Kaisarios, and dedicates the work to Alexander, son of the Moldavian prince John Mavrokordatos. The editor's life of the author at about 470-545; on the new church of Xeropotamou at about 1331; the text proper from about 1536.
